# Voyager QA Audit — Progress (shared memory across sessions)

Read this whole file first, then `qa/TEST_PLAN.md`, then the last ~20 entries of `qa/BUGS.md`.

Setup session: 2026-09-27 (Phase 0). App build: `main` @ `e3f10f5`, Flutter debug run on Windows 11 (build 26200), one display 1440×900 logical @ 200% (2880×1800 physical).

## 1. Environment

### Storage (where the data lives)

| What | Where | Synced to cloud? |
|---|---|---|
| Main database (Drift/SQLite, ~60 tables) | `%APPDATA%\Voyager\voyager\voyager.sqlite` (+ `-wal`/`-shm`) | Yes. Every user table syncs to Firestore `users/{uid}/…` through the outbox `pending_uploads_table`. |
| Images (content-addressed blobs) | `%APPDATA%\Voyager\voyager\media\` | Yes (Firebase Storage) when "Upload images to the cloud" is on; `media_assets_table.upload_state` says which |
| Auto-backups (zips + `state.json`) | `%APPDATA%\Voyager\voyager\backups\` | **No, local only** |
| Drafts / device prefs | `%APPDATA%\Voyager\voyager\finance_ui_prefs.json`, `quick_journal_entry.json`, `jobs_track_draft.json`, `leetcode_track_draft.json` | **No, local only** |
| Study/LeetCode session checkpoints (resume toast) | `%APPDATA%\Voyager\voyager\session_checkpoints\` | **No, local only** |
| Firestore SDK offline cache | `%LOCALAPPDATA%\firestore\[DEFAULT]\voyager-db9de\` | (cache of the cloud) |
| Persisted Firebase sign-in | Windows Credential Manager, generic creds `voyager-db9de.firebase.auth/[DEFAULT][0..n]` (encrypted; the e-mail can't be read offline) | n/a |
| Logs | `%USERPROFILE%\Documents\voyager_errors.log`, `perf_stall.log` (Juno keeps `perf_stall.enabled` on), `journal_debug.log`, `todo_sort_debug.log`, `sync_compare.log` | No. **Shared with Juno's own use**, so note the file size at session start and read only what was appended |
| Launch at login | `HKCU\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Run` value `Voyager` = `"C:\Users\Juno\AppData\Local\Programs\Voyager\voyager.exe" --hidden` (Juno's installed release) | No |
| Flutter run output (FlutterErrors, `[sync]` lines) | `qa/logs/run-<timestamp>.log` | n/a |

**Dev profile `qa` (QA sessions since 2026-10-12, `DEV_PROFILE_HLD.md`).** A debug Voyager run with `VOYAGER_PROFILE=qa` keeps everything apart from the normal instance above. Data, media, backups and drafts live in `%APPDATA%\Voyager\voyager-profile-qa\`. Logs go to `…\voyager-profile-qa\logs\`, not Documents. The Firestore cache is `%LOCALAPPDATA%\firestore\voyager-qa\voyager-db9de\`. The sign-in is `voyager-db9de.firebase.auth/voyager-qa[0..n]`. The app output is `qa/logs/run-qa-<timestamp>.exe.log`. A profile has its own `deviceId` and registers as `ZephyrusG14 [qa]`. It has no global hotkeys, the "Start with Windows" switch is disabled, and folder backups never run. All verified with profile `b` on 2026-10-12.

**Device-local settings.** These `settings_table` columns are left out of `settingsSyncPayload`: pane widths (journal list, dream split, edit side panel, workout library), `lastSeenNavPage`, `lastViewed{Journal,Todo,Calendar}Id`, `journalShowAllEntries`, `todoShowAllTasks`, `todoCompletedSectionExpanded`, `calendarShowAllCalendars`, LeetCode cheat-sheet tab and collapsed sections, all `dev*` flags (including `devOpenWeatherApiKey`), the weather cache, `deviceId` and `syncBackfillVersion`. Every other setting syncs.

**Local-only data check (done at setup).** Before the first wipe, Juno's local DB had outbox = 0 rows, 0 sync conflicts, and all 32 media assets `uploaded`, so nothing unsynced was lost. The local-only files (auto-backup zips, drafts/prefs JSON, session checkpoints, Documents logs) were copied to **`C:\Users\Juno\VoyagerQA-localonly-backup-2026-09-27\`**, outside the repo. Juno's local data was then wiped, as agreed.

### Harness (`qa/harness/`)

All scripts are Windows PowerShell 5.1. Run them with the **PowerShell tool** by literal path, from the repo root.

**Which instance.** Every script below acts on one Voyager instance. `-Profile <name>` names a dev profile; without it, the script uses `$env:VOYAGER_QA_PROFILE`; with neither, it uses the normal instance. `-Profile default` always means the normal instance, even while the variable is set, so a two-instance test can reach A and B in one call. The shared logic is in `instance.ps1`. PID files hold the PID and the process start time, so a PID Windows reuses after a crash is never taken for the old instance. In a profile session, **start every PowerShell call with `$env:VOYAGER_QA_PROFILE = 'qa'`**. Tool calls don't share shell state, and a bare `stop.ps1` with no profile kills every `voyager.exe`, Juno's installed release included. `voy.ps1` sends input only to the one instance's PID, so with two instances running a click can't land in the other one.

| Script | Does |
|---|---|
| `launch.ps1 [-Profile n] [-Build]` | Normal instance: `flutter run -d windows --debug` in the background. Log goes to `qa/logs/run-*.log`; waits for the VM service and writes its ws URI to `qa/logs/vm_uri.txt` and the app's PID to `pid.txt`. ~1 min with a warm build. Passes the Geoapify map key from the git-ignored `<repo>\dart_defines.json`; `-NoMapKey` leaves it out. Profile: runs the already-built `build\windows\x64\runner\Debug\voyager.exe` directly with `VOYAGER_PROFILE` set and a fixed VM service port, then runs `flutter attach` to it. The exe alone can't evaluate expressions; attach brings the compiler. Writes `vm_uri_<n>.txt` and `pid_<n>.txt`. `-Build` runs `flutter build windows --debug` first. Both the normal launch and `-Build` rebuild the debug exe, so both refuse while any debug `voyager.exe` (a profile included) is running from it. |
| `stop.ps1 [-Profile n]` | No profile: kills every `voyager.exe` (debug runs, profiles *and* the installed release) plus the `flutter run`/`flutter attach` tool processes. Leaves the Dart LSP alone. With a profile: only that profile and its attach. `-Profile default`: only the normal instance and its `flutter run`. |
| `reset.ps1 [-Profile n]` | **Baseline reset**: stop, delete the Firebase sign-in creds (= sign out), delete the data dir and the Firestore cache. Verifies each step. Touches only that instance's dir, cache and creds, and stops only that instance. |
| `sync_gate.ps1 -DataDir …` | Called by `reset.ps1` before it stops the app, signs out or deletes anything; `reset.ps1` also refuses while Voyager is still running. Throws unless the app's `sync_check.json` says nothing is unsynced **and** the database hasn't been written since that check. `reset.ps1 -Force` skips it. |
| `login.ps1 -Email voyager-qa-NNN@example.com [-SignUp] [-Password …]` | Types into the login page (maximized coordinates), then checks the account over the VM service. Refuses any non-QA e-mail. |
| `guard.ps1` | Exit 0 only if the running app is signed into `voyager-qa-*@example.com`. **Run before any destructive action.** Exit 3 = signed into another account: STOP. |
| `session_start.ps1 -Email … [-SignUp] [-Profile n]` | reset → launch (`-Build` in a profile) → login → guard. **Profile `qa` by default**; `-Profile default` uses the normal instance. |
| `session_end.ps1 [-Profile n]` | Relaunches Voyager if it crashed or was closed (so the account can be read), runs guard, waits (≤120 s) for the outbox to drain, then reset (with `-Force` only if guard confirmed a QA account) → prints `SIGNED-OUT`. Profile `qa` by default. |
| `whoami.ps1` | Offline: is a Firebase sign-in persisted for this instance? (`SIGNED-OUT` / `SIGNED-IN-UNKNOWN-ACCOUNT`) |
| `evalc.ps1 -Lib <lib> -Tag <t> -BodyFile <file> [-VmExe …]` | FV session: runs Dart statements in the running app with `c` = its ProviderContainer, to seed data through the app's own repositories and uploads. Reports `SEED-OK`/`SEED-ERR <tag>` in the run log. Example bodies in `qa/steps/fv-seed*.dart.txt`. QA accounts only; guard first. |
| `fs_snapshot.sh <uid> <out.json>` (Bash) | FV session: read-only REST snapshot of a QA account's Firestore collections (every document's `_serverWrittenAt`), to diff before/after a re-login. Since 2026-10-12 (MD-0) it covers all 62 `FirestoreCollections.records` plus `settings` (O2 of the multi-device plan); REST reads worked this time. |
| `dbdiff.py [A] [B] [--tables=a,b] [--show=N]` (Python) | 2026-10-12, multi-device plan O1: read-only row diff of two DBs (profile names or paths; default `qa qb`) by primary key, all tables but the outbox / watermarks / conflicts, all columns but media transfer state, `settings_table` on synced settings only. Version-0 rows differing only in `created_at`/`updated_at` (built-ins each device seeds locally) are listed as ignored. Since MD-1, text dates are compared as instants (a local `… -04:00` and the pulled `…Z` of the same moment are equal). Prints `DBDIFF SAME (…)` (exit 0) or per-table onlyA / onlyB / differ rows and `DBDIFF DIFF (…)` (exit 1). |
| `wait_row.py <db> "<sql>" <timeout_s>` (Python) | 2026-10-12, multi-device plan O7: polls a read-only query on a profile DB every 250 ms; `WAIT_ROW OK <s> <row>` or `WAIT_ROW TIMEOUT`. Start it in the background before the writer acts; elapsed counts from its own start. |
| `together.ps1 -A <stepsA> -B <stepsB> [-LeadMs 8000]` | 2026-10-12, multi-device MD-3 (plan §5): runs one `voy.ps1` steps file on profile `qa` and one on `qb` in two processes released at the same instant (prepends the new `voy.ps1` step `at <unix-ms>`); prints both outputs. Releases agree within ~15 ms. |
| `fs_watch.py <uid> <seconds> <doc>… [--every=0.25] [--fields=a,b]` (Python) | 2026-10-13, MD-14: read-only REST poll of a few documents under `users/<uid>/` (e.g. `sync_locks/weather_fetch weather/current`); one line per change (local time, `updateTime` or MISSING, chosen fields), then `FS_WATCH END <doc>=<changes>`. Run it in the background. |
| `httpprof.py <profile> on\|off\|clear\|get [--grep=…]` (Python, `websockets`) | 2026-10-13, MD-14: dart:io HTTP profile of one instance over its VM service. `on` starts recording (earlier requests aren't kept, so it misses a launch's warm-up); `get` lists the Cloud Function calls (default grep `cloudfunctions`) with start time and status. |
| `fs_ops.py <uid> <collection> <id> [--ops] [--chars]` (Python) | 2026-10-12, MD-3: read-only REST view of one CRDT document: its text fields, `--ops` its `sync_operations` (device, sequence, payload kind), `--chars` the live characters per `clientId` and any position two devices share (BUG-235's signature). |
| `voy.ps1` | Input + capture, background by default (below) |
| `vm.ps1 [-Profile n] whoami \| pid \| shot <png> \| eval <libSuffix> <expr> \| find [q] \| locate <n> <q> \| field \| scroll x y n` | Dart VM service client (wraps `vm.dart`; compiles it to `qa/logs/vm.exe` on first use, ~0.1 s per call after) |
| `Bg.cs` | 2026-10-12: background side of `voy.ps1` (posted messages to the FLUTTERVIEW; C# 5 like `Probe.cs`). Its header explains the modifier and mouse-leave workarounds. |
| `vimcase.ps1 <cases.tsv> [-VmExe …]` | Phase 3 field probe: Esc, set the focused field's text + caret over the VM service, send real keys, read back `selection\|mode\|length\|text` (non-ASCII as `\u{hex}`). Works on any focused text field (Vim ON or OFF). Case format in its header. |
| `Probe.cs` | Win32 side of `voy.ps1` (compiled by Add-Type; must stay C# 5: no `=>` members, no `$""`). Since Phase 5, `click`/`move`/`wheel`/`drag` also refuse a point outside Voyager's client area or covered by another window. |
| `OtherApp.cs` | Phase 5: a probe-owned WinForms "other app" (compiled with `Probe.cs` by `voy.ps1`). Verbs: `other-open [x y w h]` (opens topmost, raised by a real click, then not topmost), `other-click [dx dy]` (only if the form is the window under the point), `other-type`, `other-topmost on\|off`, `other-close`, `ostatus` (foreground owner, form text, hotkey hits, open popup menu), `other-grab <chord>` (another process owns a global hotkey), plus `tray-menu` (posted right-click → real tray menu) and `minimize`. The form lives only for one `voy.ps1` run. Since FV: a second form B (`otherb-open x y w h`, `otherb-click`, `otherb-close`) and `zorder` (top-to-bottom order of A, B and Voyager's main window). |

### SESSION START (procedure)

1. `& "C:\Users\Juno\Code\Voyager\qa\harness\session_start.ps1" -Email voyager-qa-NNN@example.com` (add `-SignUp` for a new account; take the next unused number from the account table below and add it there).
   - **Since 2026-10-12 this runs in dev profile `qa`** (Storage above). It wipes and signs out only the `qa` profile, rebuilds the debug exe from the working tree, launches it beside whatever Juno is running, and signs the QA account in. Juno's install is never stopped, signed out or wiped, and the sync gate never applies to Juno's data. The rebuild fails while any *debug* `voyager.exe` is running; Juno's installed release doesn't block it. From here on, start every PowerShell call with `$env:VOYAGER_QA_PROFILE = 'qa'` (Harness above).
   - A profile has no global hotkeys, no "Start with Windows" and no folder backups. For a phase that tests those, run the old way with `-Profile default` (on both `session_start.ps1` and `session_end.ps1`). That fully quits Voyager (including Juno's installed release), signs out whatever account is persisted (Juno signs the real account back in between sessions), wipes all of the normal instance's local data, launches the debug build and signs the QA account in.
   - **Never type Juno's real credentials, never click "Continue with Google", never modify the real account.**
   - (Normal instance only.) If Juno's real account was used since the last session, the reset refuses until Juno has run Dev page → **Check, then quit (before a wipe)** in Voyager. Only that check proves nothing would be lost; an empty outbox doesn't (the 2026-09-27 wipe). Don't bypass it with `-Force`: tell Juno.
2. It must end with `GUARD OK: voyager-qa-NNN@example.com`. Anything else: stop.
3. Reusing an account: its data comes back from its cloud copy through the startup pull. Wait ~10 s, then check the expected rows in SQLite (read-only) before relying on them.
4. Check the **standard test configuration** (TEST_PLAN.md): Juno's background settings are the app defaults since 2026-09-28, so a fresh account already has them. Check `settings_table.theme_mode='dark'`, `geometric_wave_scatter_mode=1`, `geometric_wave_enabled=0`, `geometric_texture_scale=15.0`. An account created before that date holds the old defaults: reset them in Dev → Geometric texture / wave tuning → Reset to defaults (petals by hand), or use a fresh account.
5. Note the current sizes of the `Documents\*.log` files, so you only read what this session appends.
6. If sign-up ever asks for e-mail verification, **stop and tell Juno**. Do not work around it. (At setup it didn't: sign-up lands straight on Journal.)

### SESSION END (procedure). Run it even if the phase stopped early

1. `& "C:\Users\Juno\Code\Voyager\qa\harness\session_end.ps1"` (add `-Profile default` if the session ran in the normal instance): lets the outbox drain so the QA account's cloud copy is complete, quits the QA instance, signs it out, wipes its local data. It must print `SIGNED-OUT`.
2. Local data is wiped at the end on purpose. In the normal instance the local DB isn't per-account, so leftover QA rows could merge into Juno's real account when they sign back in on this PC. In profile `qa` the wipe keeps the next session's start clean.
3. After a normal-instance session, Juno signs back in themselves. Don't do it for them.

### BASELINE RESET (procedure)

`reset.ps1`, then `launch.ps1`, then `login.ps1`; in one go, that's `session_start.ps1`. Tested at setup (2026-09-27), in order: reset from Juno's live state → launch → sign-up qa-001 → session_end → session_start qa-001 (sign-in path) → guard OK → session_end → `SIGNED-OUT`.
"Baseline" = a brand-new account (`-SignUp`) on an empty local DB. A reused account is baseline plus whatever that account put in its cloud copy.

**Mid-session reset without changing account** (e.g. "does it survive a restart?"): `stop.ps1`, then `launch.ps1` (with `$env:VOYAGER_QA_PROFILE = 'qa'` set in that call, in a profile session). The persisted sign-in and local DB survive. For a cold-cache restart of the same account (a full pull from the cloud), run `session_start.ps1 -Email <same>`.

### Background mode (default since 2026-10-12): don't take Juno's screen

Every `voy.ps1` run starts in `mode bg`: input is posted straight to Voyager's window (`Bg.cs`), so Juno keeps using the PC while you test. Start a session with `voy.ps1 do "park"` (window off-screen, client 2880×1800 = the maximized coordinates below, never activated). `unpark` puts it back at (0,0). Verified 2026-10-12 with Voyager parked and Juno working in other apps: the foreground never changed.

- **Prefer widget targets over coordinates:** `find [text]` lists clickable texts / tooltips / ValueKeys with centre coords; `tap Save`, `tap tip:Delete entry`, `tap key:shell.search.results`, `tap Edit #2` (2nd match), `tap X right`. A failed `tap` lists similar labels, marking hidden ones NOT-CLICKABLE (pages the shell keeps alive, text under a dialog, scrolled out of view). No app changes were needed; only icon-only buttons with no tooltip need coordinates.
- **Works in bg:** `click` (incl. right, N clicks), `type` (real key events, so Vim sees them; capitals/symbols too), `key` chords (Ctrl/Shift/Alt), `drag` (posted as one instant burst), `wheel` (sent as an in-app scroll event: posted WM_MOUSEWHEEL lands at the real cursor), `shot` (VM frame grab), `field` (focused field `selection|vimMode|length|text`, newlines and non-ASCII escaped), `close`. Posted input and frame grabs also worked with the PC locked (2026-10-10), so the screen saver doesn't block bg runs; the modifier workaround hasn't been tried while locked yet.
- **Needs `mode fg`** (real input, takes Juno's screen; `activate` also switches to it): hover, long-press, slow/timed drags, focus/blur and "another app" tests (`other-*`), global `hotkey`, `place`/`maximize`/`restore`/`minimize`, and `tray-open`/`tray-menu`/`tray-quit` (they activate Voyager). `voy.ps1` refuses these in bg. Batch fg work and tell Juno before it.
- **Vim trap:** `key esc` leaves a field in Normal mode, where typing runs commands and Backspace does nothing; a drag enters Visual. Check `field` before reading a no-op as an app bug; `key shift+a` re-enters Insert at the end.
- Each bg run resets the system sleep timer, so the PC doesn't sleep mid-session (the display still can).

### Interaction method (verified 2026-09-27, session unlocked)

**Real input into the native window via Win32 (`voy.ps1` + `Probe.cs`), with guards.** No UI Automation: Flutter exposes no semantics tree here. No integration_test either: that is a test suite, not a manual pass.

- `voy.ps1 run <steps.txt>` (preferred) or `voy.ps1 do "step; step; …"` runs a whole scenario in **one process**. Each separate tool call can take focus away from the app, so put a whole scenario in one run. Step verbs: `activate`, `key <chord>`, `type <text>`, `click x y [right] [N]`, `move`, `wheel x y notches`, `drag x1 y1 x2 y2`, `hotkey <chord>`, `wait ms`, `shot <name>`, `status`, `place x y w h`, `maximize`, `restore`, `close`, `tray-open`, `tray-quit`. The header of `voy.ps1` documents them. `do` splits on `;`, so text containing `;` needs `run`.
- **Guards:** every key/click is refused unless the foreground window belongs to `voyager.exe`. `hotkey` is only sent if some process has registered that global hotkey. Screenshots capture only Voyager's client area (`PrintWindow`), never the screen.
- **Coordinates are physical client pixels**, the same as the screenshot PNG (2880×1800 maximized). The image viewer shows these PNGs scaled to 2000×1250, so **multiply displayed coords by 1.44**. Rail items at x=88: Journal y=298, Dreams 418, To-Do 538, Calendar 658, Search 778, Analytics 898, Finance 1018, Life 1138, LeetCode 1258, Rankings 1378, Jobs 1498 (the rest scroll below; inbox tray icon ≈ y 1688).
- **Verified for real at setup:**

| Check | Result |
|---|---|
| Launch (`launch.ps1`) | Worked |
| Click (the login page's "Create account", fields, rail items) | Worked |
| Type (e-mail and password, journal body text) | Worked; real VK presses, shift for `@` |
| App shortcut `Ctrl+/` | Opened the shortcuts dialog |
| `Esc` | Closed the dialog |
| Global hotkey `Ctrl+Alt+T` with main focused | In-app path: navigated to To-Do |
| Resize (`place`) | Worked (clamped to the 1440×1040 min) |
| Maximize | Worked |
| WM_CLOSE | Window hid to the tray |
| Tray "Open Voyager" message | Window back and foreground |
| Screenshot, PrintWindow | Worked |
| Screenshot, VM-service frame grab | Worked, identical output |
| `vm.ps1 whoami` | Worked |

- **VM service** (`vm.ps1`): `whoami`; `shot <png>` renders the Flutter layer tree, so it works when covered or locked; `eval <libSuffix> <expr>` evaluates in one library's scope. Expressions are collapsed to one line; wrap state changes in `Future(() => …)`. GoRouter navigation by eval (see memory notes in AUDIT_TESTING.md) was **not** verified in this harness. Use rail clicks / `Ctrl+Tab`.
- **Data checks:** read-only SQLite from Python: `sqlite3.connect('file:%APPDATA%\\Voyager\\voyager\\voyager.sqlite?mode=ro', uri=True)`. The app has a 5 s busy_timeout, so reads while it runs are safe.

**Limitations**
- If the PC is **locked** (`voy.ps1 status` shows `locked=True`), real input and PrintWindow are unavailable. Don't use `mode fg`; background mode (above) still works.
- "Another app has focus" scenarios (floater blur/click-outside) need a probe-owned WinForms window (pattern in `AUDIT_TESTING.md` Session 2). Not built into this harness yet.
- Native dialogs (file picker for backup Export/Import) are Win32 common dialogs outside Flutter. Drive them with keyboard (type a full path + Enter) only while their window belongs to voyager.exe; `Probe` guards on process, so this works.
- No admin rights: no firewall-based offline testing. Use **Dev page → Force offline** (session-only) for offline states. Since 2026-10-03 it cuts the app off for real, not just the badge: Firestore's network is disabled (`disableNetwork()`), and the connectivity probe, media upload/download/delete, Geoapify place search / reverse geocode / Google Maps short links and map tiles all fail as an unreachable host would. Writes then behave as on a real outage: the upload hangs, times out after the write gate's 45 s, and lands in `pending_uploads_table`; turning it off re-enables Firestore and the outbox drains. Not cut: weather and sign-in. Verified on qa-010 (see its row). Driven by eval, the toggle is `DevFlags.forceOffline = v; c.read(devForceOfflineProvider.notifier).state = v; await c.read(firestoreProvider).disableNetwork();` (`enableNetwork()` to undo) with `-Lib features/dev/dev_page.dart`. Setting `DevFlags.forceOffline` alone still leaves Firestore online.
- Minimum window is 720×520 logical (1440×1040 physical). The compact/bottom-nav shell (<600 logical wide) is **unreachable on desktop**.
- Multi-monitor / mixed DPI: only one display attached.

---

## 5. Environment quirks

- **Windows PowerShell 5.1:** `.ps1` files must be ASCII (a BOM-less UTF-8 em dash broke parsing). Native stderr + `$ErrorActionPreference='Stop'` throws, which is why `vm.ps1` wraps `dart run`.
- **Shell guard hook (dcg):** `>` redirects need literal paths (not `$var`). No `perl -pi`. Don't invoke scripts through a variable (`& $s`) in multi-line commands. Don't write scripts with Bash heredocs. Write files with the Write tool, then run them by literal path. `git checkout -- <path>` is blocked.
- **Focus:** `activate` sometimes needs its fallbacks (SwitchToThisWindow, then minimize/restore). A minimize/restore can itself trigger window-state handlers, so note it if a test is about window state.
- **Login card re-centres** when toggling Sign in ↔ Create account (fields move down ~56 px). `login.ps1` handles it. **It also moves when an error line appears** (one line: Email y≈696, Password y≈816 in sign-in mode; sign-up mode with one error line: 752/872). `login.ps1` assumes no error is showing: after a failed attempt, click the fields yourself. Dialogs (e.g. Change password) also grow and re-centre when an error appears, so re-measure button positions from a screenshot.
- **Settings reopens on its last tab.** To sign out, click the Account tab (282, 46) first, then Sign out (394, 451). Step file `qa/steps/p1-signout.txt` does the whole thing (rail scroll → Settings → Account → Sign out).
- **Rail when scrolled** (after `wheel 88 1400 -10`): Study ≈ y 1158, Workout ≈ 1280, Settings ≈ 1518, and Search/Analytics/Finance move up (Finance ≈ 569).
- **`vm.ps1` changes the working directory** of the PowerShell session; use absolute paths after calling it.
- **Harness-assisted helpers** (scratchpad, recreate if needed): read-only SQLite query script, and a PIL contact-sheet/crop script to tile several screenshots into one image for review.
- **Mid-session account switch:** `reset.ps1` without `-Force` refuses while Voyager runs. After `guard.ps1` shows a QA account, `reset.ps1 -Force` → `launch.ps1` → `login.ps1` is the allowed cold re-login.
- **Debug build:** first frames and page switches are slower than release. The shell warm-up of hidden pages is **off in debug**, so a first visit builds on arrival. Don't log debug-only slowness as a bug unless it's severe; note "debug build".
- **Documents logs** are shared with Juno's everyday use, and `perf_stall.log` keeps growing during QA. Read only what this session appended.
- **Installed release** (`%LOCALAPPDATA%\Programs\Voyager\voyager.exe`) starts at Windows login with `--hidden` and shares the same data dir. `stop.ps1` kills it too. Never test against it.
- Screenshots are 2880×1800. The viewer downsizes by 1.44; click coordinates are physical. **If you resize a shot yourself (e.g. PIL to 1440×900), the factor is 2, not 1.44** (Phase 10: several misses came from this, one of which saved a stray event by clicking outside the popover).
- `dart run qa/harness/vm.dart` prints "Running build hooks..." (harmless; filtered by `vm.ps1`).
- **Cold re-login of the same account:** `session_start.ps1 -Email <same>` (no `-SignUp`) is refused by the sync gate ("no sync check has been run") unless the Dev page check was run. Use the allowed path instead: app running → `guard.ps1` OK (+ outbox 0) → `reset.ps1 -Force` → `launch.ps1` → `login.ps1 -Email …`.
- **Dev page** is a nav page hidden by default: Settings → Appearance → Navigation pages → eye icon on "Dev" → Save. It then sits above Settings on the rail. Force offline toggle ≈ (2739, 776) maximized.
- **Settings tabs** (maximized, y=46): Account 282, Appearance 487, Editing 685, Pages 841. Weather location is on **Pages**; Vim toggle on Editing ≈ (2739, 258); Theme Dark/Light on Appearance ≈ (2525, 829) / (2707, 829); Navigation pages row ≈ (1500, 1570).
- **Floaters:** a global hotkey sent while the window is hidden turns the main window into the floater. Esc and the same hotkey don't dismiss it (by design); `tray-open` brings the main window back with its placement.
- **Writing to BUGS.md:** the dcg hook can misfire on Python heredocs whose text contains flag-like strings; use the Edit tool for BUGS.md entries.
- **Fast VM probes:** `dart compile exe qa/harness/vm.dart -o <scratchpad>\vm.exe` once per session. Each call then takes ~0.6 s instead of several seconds. Run it from the repo root (it reads `qa/logs/vm_uri.txt`). The VM service truncates string results at ~128 chars, so put short fields first in any expression that returns text.
- **VM-service writes to a text controller** (`controller.value = …`) can log "Build scheduled during frame" into `voyager_errors.log` (stack frame `Eval`). That's the harness, not an app bug.
- **The probe can't type a backtick:** on this PC's keyboard layout (`0409:00060409`), `Probe.cs` `type` sends an `r` for a backtick. Use the clipboard for text containing one.
- **`vimcase.ps1` fixture writes are deferred** (`Future`), so a synchronous write no longer trips the debug `StackFrame` assertion. Put `{wait:1500}` before an edit you plan to undo.
- **Emoji in text fields with Vim ON crash the app** (BUG-015). Don't leave emoji in fixtures you'll edit with Vim motions unless you're testing that.
- **The dcg hook** also blocks Python heredocs whose text contains Markdown backticks ("embedded shell launcher"), and `python … "${var}…"` paths in PowerShell. Use the Edit tool for Markdown, and literal or relative paths for scripts.
- **Don't click the bottom-right corner when maximized.** The Finance "+" FAB (≈2791, 1711) sits under the auto-hidden taskbar's clock; the click opens the Windows notification panel (ShellExperienceHost), which then keeps the foreground, and `activate`, `tray-open` and even a relaunch can't take it back. Use a non-maximized window (`place 200 100 2000 1100`) for bottom-edge controls.
- **Dialogs re-centre as they grow** (dictionary errors, snippet rows): re-measure from a screenshot before each click. The dictionary dialog also drops focus after a rejected Enter (BUG-026), so click the field again before typing.
- **Image clipboard:** Voyager reads only the registered "PNG"/JPEG/WebP/HEIC clipboard formats. `Set-Clipboard -Path` (a file drop) and .NET `SetImage` alone (bitmap) are ignored. Use a DataObject with the image *and* `SetData('PNG', $false, <MemoryStream of the PNG bytes>)`; the Phase 4 helper did exactly that (scratch `setimg.ps1`, recreate if needed). Test images are in `qa/data/`.
- **Programmatic fixture writes don't autosave** (`vimcase.ps1` sets `controller.value`, which fires no `onChanged`): the DB keeps the last *typed* body. Type at least one character if a check depends on the saved value.
- **vimcase keys are space-separated tokens**: a literal space must be `{space}` (`ld** tail` types "ld**tail").
- **At 2000×1100 the rail shows fewer items and the inbox sits lower**; navigate with Ctrl+Tab / Ctrl+Shift+Tab instead of rail coordinates (Journal ↔ Settings wrap).
- **A wrong click coordinate once reached another app** (Phase 5): a point read off a downscaled contact sheet was outside the 1344×239 floater, and the click landed on Juno's IDE window (one left click, no keys; the next `type` was refused). `Probe.cs` now refuses clicks outside Voyager's client area. Measure coordinates on the unscaled shot, and remember floaters are small windows.
- **Probe form vs. foreground:** after `other-open`, `ostatus` often shows `fg=explorer:Shell_TrayWnd` (the taskbar) instead of the form; the floater still opens and dismisses correctly, and focus returns there. With the main window maximized it covers the form: `place 200 100 2000 1100` first, or the guard refuses `other-click`. End a run with `activate` (or leave a window foreground): when the probe process exits while its form is foreground, the foreground becomes empty and `activate` reports `locked=True`.
- **Posted tray clicks can't grant foreground:** `tray-open` sometimes leaves Voyager visible but not foreground, and a tray menu opened by `tray-menu` doesn't close on clicking elsewhere (AUDIT finding 7 can't be judged this way). Close it with `activate; key esc`.
- **Floater coordinates** (client px): to-do bar 1344×127: title (500,62), Due (1075,62), List (1240,62); the date picker grows it to 987 tall, the list picker to 239 (first list row ≈ y 175). Finance 1264×1049: amount (632,286), Add (632,917). Reminder 952×1023: title (475,167), time chip (250,552), Create (865,930) with the validation line showing, (865,929) without. Notepad 744×631: Delete (690,59).
- **Hotkeys are registered once at launch.** If a probe or anything else holds a combo at launch, Voyager never gets it back until restart (BUG-034); `voy.ps1 hotkey` then refuses to send it.
- **Tab focus probe:** `vm.ps1 eval "features/shell/app_shell.dart" "FocusManager.instance.primaryFocus?.toStringShort() ?? 'none'"` shows where keyboard focus is (focus rings are invisible in the app, BUG-009).
- **Verifying OS toasts (P6):** read Windows' notification store read-only: `sqlite3.connect('file:<LOCALAPPDATA>/Microsoft/Windows/Notifications/wpndatabase.db?mode=ro', uri=True)`, join `Notification.HandlerId` → `NotificationHandler.RecordId` where `PrimaryId = 'Voyager.App'`, regex `<text>` out of `Payload`; `ArrivalTime` is a FILETIME. It also holds Juno's own notifications: filter on Voyager only. Windows keeps 20 per app. Toasts are never withdrawn on ack on Windows (by design), and a re-fire of the same source replaces its toast.
- **Toast Undo has an 8 s dwell:** reading a screenshot takes longer, so click Undo in the same `voy.ps1` run, or `move` the mouse onto the toast right away (hover holds it) and read its position from a shot.
- **`key A` sends an unshifted `a`:** for Vim capitals use `type A`, not `key A`.
- **The Inbox popover re-lays out** after every dismiss/add (it's anchored at the bottom and grows upward): re-measure the broom, `+` and row positions from a fresh shot. Clicking a pinned-note row opens its inline editor (Esc commits the edit).
- **Rail scroll persists:** after visiting Dev/Settings the rail may stay scrolled (To-Do at y 538 becomes Finance). `wheel 88 600 10` first.
- **Don't `maximize` right after `launch.ps1`.** The app maximizes itself during startup; a harness `maximize` sent while that runs can leave the window zoomed at the centred restore position (`status` shows `zoomed=True rect=160,100 2906x1826` instead of `-13,-13`). Then a native caption strip appears along the top on hover and swallows clicks there (journal header dropdown, flag, gear): this was FV-2's "dropdown won't open" lead. Seen 1 in 4 with the harness `maximize`, 0 in 6 without. Check `status` after a launch; if the rect is wrong, `restore` then `maximize` fixes it.
- **Drag-and-drop (`dragdrop.ps1 -Files <paths> -X <clientX> -Y <clientY>`):** opens the drag-source form at screen (2380,1400); place Voyager away from it (`place 200 100 2000 1100`). It activates Voyager, presses on the form, glides to the point and releases only if the window there belongs to voyager.exe (once it was Juno's IDE and the drag was cancelled). A 12 s safety timeout closes the form. Output: `owner@target=… effect=Copy|None`.
- **Journal header/journal-row coordinates (maximized):** dropdown (340,55), gear/Manage journals (724,55); Manage dialog rows ⋮ at x≈1820–1832 (rows re-centre as the count changes: re-measure); New entry button (486,1736); editor trash (2791,252), delete confirm (1627,985), toast Undo ≈ (1663,65); OTD tucked strip (2847,500). Journal order in the dropdown isn't stable across installs (BUG-056), so re-read it from a shot.
- **`evalc.ps1` seed bodies must be pure ASCII.** It reads the body with PowerShell 5.1 `Get-Content -Raw` (ANSI), so UTF-8 literals arrive as mojibake. The Write tool turns `\uXXXX` escapes into literal characters, so either build strings with `String.fromCharCodes([...])` in the body or put non-ASCII text on the clipboard and paste it through the UI.
- **Seeding through a repository doesn't refresh open pages.** Dreams seeded with `evalc.ps1` appeared only after a restart (the page's provider isn't invalidated by a raw `upsertEntry`). Harness artifact, not a bug.
- **Dreams page coordinates (maximized, default list width 480 logical):** New dream (662, 1735), rows start y≈175 and step ≈168 (re-measure: the list re-sorts by date), editor trash (2782, 239), delete confirm (1633, 985), toast Undo ≈ (1663, 65), date pill ≈ (1238, 239), sticky-note tab (2782, 1703), note field (2560, 1520), note close (2793, 1371), split divider at x ≈ 1145 at the default width (re-measure from a shot after a drag). With `dream_split_width` 390.5 the New dream button is at (582, 1735).
- **Direct Firestore REST reads (`gcloud` token + curl) were blocked** by the session's permission classifier in Phase 8. `fs_snapshot.sh` may hit the same block; plan checks against the local DB, or ask Juno first.
- **Tab + Enter can minimize or hide Voyager** (BUG-086): Tab walks into the invisible title-bar Minimize/Maximize/Close buttons. In a harness run, don't press Enter after several Tabs without checking `primaryFocus` (`qa/steps/kf.ps1`). If the window vanished, `tray-open` (hidden) or `activate` (minimized) brings it back; after `tray-open` focus sits on the root scope and Tab does nothing until something is clicked.

- **The screen saver starts after 5 minutes without input** (`ScreenSaveTimeOut` 300, Mystify, not password-protected). While it runs there is no foreground window, so `voy.ps1 status` shows `locked=True`, `activate` fails and PrintWindow shots fail; `vm.exe shot` still works. Phase 17 hit this during a 6-minute idle wait across midnight, and Juno had to dismiss it. Don't stop the screen saver process yourself. For a long idle wait, keep input going inside the same `voy.ps1` run (`move x y; wait 120000; move …`) on a spot that doesn't rebuild what is being watched, or wait with `vm.exe shot` only and expect to need Juno afterwards.

Trash dialog (maximized): Close (1986, 1421), Empty trash (1917, 379), first row's Restore (1853, 736) with three chip rows / (1853, 657) with two / (1853, 577) with one, ⋯ at x 1977; the chip rows reflow as features empty, so re-measure after every restore. Its "Restored …" toasts never leave (BUG-051): restart before a screenshot-heavy check.
- **(P24) Seeding / deleting over the VM service doesn't refresh open pages or an open Trash dialog;** end a body with `invalidateAllDataProvidersIn(c)` and reopen the Trash. `evalc.ps1` throws on vm.exe's stderr (any compile error); the scratch `evalq.ps1` reports the error and its position instead. Seed bodies are joined into one line, so a `//` comment swallows the rest. Double fields (`interval`, `intValue`, `weightKg`) need `3.0` not `3`.
- **(P24) A `stop.ps1` right after an eval kills its unfinished Future:** the 3-item bucket seed stopped after the first item. Wait for `SEED-OK` (scratch `tl.ps1`) before stopping.
- **(P24) GlassButtons ignore Enter / Space (BUG-207):** keyboard passes that land on a glass button can't press it; Tab order is still readable with `tabwalk.ps1`.

Settings → Pages → `wheel 1500 900 -20` puts Grade: Fail / Hard / Good / Easy at y 335 / 463 / 591 / 718 (x 350); Editing tab (684, 45), Vim switch (2438, 258); Appearance (487, 45), Dark / Light (2220 / 2403, 862); Data (978, 45), Trash row (800, 1050), first Restore (1702, 412), Close (1834, 1336). Arrow keys in `voy.ps1` are `key left` / `key right` (not `arrowleft`).
- **Toast stacking moves Undo:** the resume toast (10 s), a stuck Trash "Restored" toast (BUG-051) and a delete toast stack top-centre; the delete toast's Undo was at (1427, 190) with the resume toast up and (1486, 200) below a stuck toast. Shoot first; a click after the stack shifts lands on the card and flips it.
- **Study checkpoints** live in `%APPDATA%\Voyager\voyager\session_checkpoints\` as `studySession__deck_<id>.json`, `studySession__hub.json`, `studyCram__deck_<id>.json` (version, sourceIds, remainingQueue / buckets, graded / undone steps); read them with Python to check a resume.
- **`voy.ps1 wheel x y N` sends one WM_MOUSEWHEEL of N×120**, which Flutter's notch handler counts as a single notch: number wheels (workout, time pickers) move one row per event. Send N separate `wheel x y -1; wait 50` steps to move N rows (135 lb from 0 = 54 notches).
- **Never restart Voyager with a workout in progress** (BUG-198): the relaunched app takes no clicks or keys until the workout ends. Finish or discard it first; if stuck, end it with scratch `ws.ps1` (`…finish()`), which restores input at once.
- **Workout (maximized):** toolbar Week (249, 65) / Split (357, 65), Start today (1188, 65), History (973, 65) (hidden under the island in Split while a workout is live); weekday column centres x 373 / 687 / 1001 / 1315 / 1629 / 1943 / 2257, column ▶ at (x + 114, 176); first card (x, 282), second (x, 397); card menu items Edit target +(250, 51) / Edit sets and drops +(250, 131) / Remove +(250, 211); library cards x 2635 from y 269, step 100; library + (2788, 180). Target editor: weight wheel (1348, 827), reps (1564, 827), Sets (1484, 1161). Live view: island (1440, 62), collapse caret (992, 79), Finish (1798, 79) → confirm Finish (1930, 985), trash (1904, 79) → Delete (2056, 985); weight wheel (1348, 485); Complete set (1440, 1247), **(1440, 1289) when the "Planned … just for today" line shows**; Sets field (1444, 975). Settings → Pages → Workout section after `wheel 1500 900 -10` ×4: lb/kg (1828/2743, 1148), rest switch (2739, 1276), slider left end (304, 1447).
- **(P25) Native file dialogs** (Export / Import Backup, Save a copy): after the click, `key ctrl+a; type <full path>; key enter` in the same `voy.ps1` run works. If foreground drifts away between tool calls (it went to Juno's IDE twice), `voy.ps1` refuses input; use the scratch `dlgbtn.ps1 -Title "Export Backup" -Button Cancel` (BM_CLICK on Voyager's own dialog) instead of fighting for the foreground. Windows answers an unwritable path or a read-only file in its own message box ("Confirm Save As" → Yes → an OK box), stacked over the save dialog: close all of them.
- **(P25) Settings → Data coordinates (maximized):** Export Backup (418, 1548), Import Backup (418, 1678), Automatic backups switch (2739, 1307), status row (700, 1420), Image storage (500, 840), Download images switch (2739, 584); restore confirm's Restore (2451, 985); backup list: newest row's ⋯ (1958, 559) → Restore… (1765, 565) / Save a copy… (1765, 655), Show in folder (1836, 439). Appearance (scrolled to top): accent tile (500, 220), Light (2707, 829), Wave (2739, 1229), Scatter (2739, 1357), Navigation pages (500, 1571), Startup page (500, 1700); nav dialog eye column x 1623, rows from y 225 step 96, Save (1709, 1675), Cancel (1583, 1675). Pages (scrolled to top): quotes switch (2739, 228), weather field (1500, 572) / Save location (2677, 684), Hide completed (2739, 962–1010, re-shoot). Editing: Vim (2739, 258), Caps Lock (2739, 400), Autocorrect (2739, 543), Start with Windows (2739, 1704) after `wheel 1500 900 -30`.
- **(P25) The rail moves with the Dev page and the offline badge:** with Dev shown and Force offline on, Settings sat at (88, 1467) and the old (88, 1555) hit the inbox; a scripted import then clicked Dev-page switches by mistake (two flags toggled and put back). Shoot the rail before scripted navigation.
- **(P25) The background pauses under any dialog** (`TickerMode` off while a modal scrim shows): two shots with a dialog open are pixel-identical. Diff whole frames, not a strip, when checking that it animates.
- **(P26) After a `stop.ps1` kill, read-only SQLite fails** ("attempt to write a readonly database": the WAL needs recovery). Copy `voyager.sqlite` + `-wal` + `-shm` to the scratchpad and query the copy, or relaunch first.
- **(P26) dcg blocks** bash `for … $(…)` loops (write a Python poller), heredocs containing backticks (use the Edit tool for BUGS.md entries), and `powershell -File` (call `.ps1` scripts with `&`). PowerShell tool calls refuse `Start-Sleep` ≥ ~60 s in a chain; keep long waits inside one `voy.ps1 do "… wait 30000; move …"` run.
- **(P26) Dev page coordinates (maximized, after `wheel 88 1400 -10; click 88 1400`):** from the top, `wheel 1500 900 -5` puts Verbose sync at (2739, 134), Force offline (2739, 275), Show local save / upload / download (2739, 1313 / 1441 / 1570). `wheel 1500 900 -50` from the top: Check every collection (2822, 349), Compare all journal entries (2822, 606), Compare all todo lists (2822, 734), Compare todo list with remote (2822, 975), View compare log (2822, 1202). A whole-journal compare of 299 entries takes ~55 s. The FPS overlay sits over the FPS switch, which still takes clicks.
- **(P26) A journal entry opened in the editor keeps its text against live remote changes** (BUG-220): for any "remote change" simulation, use an entry not opened in this run, or expect the stale row.
- **(FV) Probe forms open *under* a foreground Voyager window:** `other-open` / `otherb-open` raise their form with a click at its centre, and the guard refuses if Voyager covers that point. Place Voyager clear of the forms first (FV-7 used `place 1000 600 1800 1100` with A at the default 60,60 900×500 and B at 200,300 900×500); `zorder` then reads the real stack.
- **(FV) Settings → Data rows moved again:** a "Folder backups" row (new) sits above Export. Maximized, scrolled to the top: Trash (374, 1184); after `wheel 1500 900 -10`: Trash (374, 1064), automatic-backups status row (648, 1325), Export (446, 1580), Import (446, 1708). Trash dialog ⋯ (1977, 497) → "Delete forever" (2070, 578) → confirm (1948, 985). Once the confirm click closed the Trash without erasing; check SQL after every erase.
- **(FV) Before any wipe, check what's running:** on 2026-10-04 Juno had a `flutter run` debug Voyager of their own signed into the real account. `whoami.ps1` (SIGNED-IN-UNKNOWN-ACCOUNT) plus `sync_watermarks_table.user_id` against the QA uids told it apart; the session waited for Juno's "Check, then quit".
- **Reminder editor via Ctrl+Alt+R** (in-app) opens centred with fixed positions at maximized: time chip (1188, 937), Create (1837, 1346) with the "Fires in…" line. Typing `h:mm AM` + Enter in the picker's time field sets it and closes the picker; clicking outside the editor discards it silently.

- **(MD-0) Who owns a running debug `voyager.exe`:** `qa/logs/pid.txt` (no suffix) is a normal-instance launch, `pid_<name>.txt` a profile. On 2026-10-12 a normal-instance debug run of Juno's (uid `9nlbRSp0…`, no QA account) was up and blocked `session_start.ps1`'s build; Juno quit it on request. Ask, never stop it.
- **(MD-0) `session_start.ps1 -Profile qa` may print no build output:** `flutter build windows --debug` is incremental, so `voyager.exe` can keep an old timestamp. Judge freshness by `build\windowsd
unner\Debug\datalutter_assets\kernel_blob.bin` against the newest `lib/**/*.dart`.
- **(MD-0) Verbose sync is session-only** (`DevFlags.verboseSync`, not stored): re-enable it after every launch, on each instance: `evalc.ps1 -Profile qa|qb -Lib features/dev/dev_page.dart -Tag v -BodyFile qa\steps\md-verbose-on.dart.txt` (no need to show the Dev page).
- **(MD-0) `reset.ps1 -Profile qb` re-registers B as a new device** on its next sign-in (new data dir, new `deviceId`); the old registration stays, so the Devices list grows by one "ZephyrusG14 [qb]" per reset. It also leaves an empty `%LOCALAPPDATA%irestoreoyager-qb` folder (the cache inside it is gone).
- **(MD-1) A device that pulled a date stores it as UTC (`…Z`), the device that wrote it as local time with an offset (`… -04:00`)** in Drift's text columns: the same instant. `dbdiff.py` compares them as instants since 2026-10-12; any other raw comparison of the two DBs has to do the same.
- **(MD-1) `evalc.ps1` libraries for the FV seeds:** `fv-seed1/2` and anything with journals/finance/workout/jobs: `features/trash/trash_item_detail.dart`; `fv-seed3a-reminder`: `features/notifications/scheduled_reminders_section.dart`; `fv-seed3b-inbox`: `features/notifications/notification_inbox_popover.dart`; `fv-seed3c-quote`: `features/settings/settings_page.dart`; the full check: `features/dev/dev_sync_compare_tile.dart`. A wrong library fails to compile with only a `vm.dart:56` stack on stderr. The uid: `vm.ps1 -Profile x eval app/providers.dart "FirebaseAuth.instanceFor(app: voyagerFirebaseApp).currentUser?.uid"` (plain `FirebaseAuth.instance` has no `[DEFAULT]` app in a profile).
- **(MD-1) A joining device seeds its built-ins only when their page first opens** (starters/plans on Workout, stages/companies on Jobs), so O1 right after a join lists them as onlyA. Open Workout and Jobs on B before trusting a diff.
- **(MD-1) The command-safety hook blocks some one-liners** (a PowerShell hashtable plus `foreach` was read as a git branch delete; a bash heredoc into python was read as a truncating redirect; inline `python -c` with nested quotes fails in PowerShell). Use plain sequential calls, scripts in the scratchpad, and `qa/steps/md-q.py <profiles> "<sql>"`.
- **(MD-2) The MD-2 driver** (`qa/steps/md2.py <writer> <tag> <lib> <body> --sql … [--find … --absent] [--n run]`): runs an evalc-style body on the writer, times the receiver's row and on-screen text from the body's `MARK;`, appends to `qa/exports/md2-latency.tsv`. `md2cycle.py <spec.tsv> <writer> <n>` runs a feature's spec; `md2gen.py` regenerates the per-feature create/edit/delete bodies + specs; `md2page.py <RailLabel> <spec> <n>` navigates both and runs A→B then B→A; `md2lat.py` prints the O7 table. `--n` can be any string (words with digits are refused by the dictionary: use letter-only names there). Placeholders `@W@` (writer) and `@N@` (run). Don't name body variables `e`, `v`, `c` or `r`: `e`/`v`/`c` are the harness's walker, `r` is the remote by convention.
- **(MD-2) `vm find` can't hit-test some pages' texts** (Analytics trackers, Jobs stage names): set `MD2_ANYTEXT=1` for `md2.py` to look in the widget tree instead (`locate`'s similar list); that also sees hidden kept-alive pages, so keep the receiver on the page.
- **(MD-2) The receiver only shows what its current scope shows:** a new journal / list / calendar on the writer is invisible on a receiver that has another one selected. Put both on All journals / All tasks / All calendars (switchers: journal (340, 55), to-do (470, 55), calendar (770, 65)) before UI checks.
- **(MD-2) Eval libraries:** most records `features/trash/trash_item_detail.dart`; contribution rooms / room events need `features/finance/finance_analytics_view.dart`; pinned notes `features/notifications/notification_inbox_popover.dart`; rules / entity reminders `features/notifications/scheduled_reminders_section.dart`; dictionary / snippets / quotes `features/settings/settings_page.dart`; `completeTodoTask` `features/todo/todo_edit_panel.dart`; jobs actions `features/jobs/jobs_page.dart`; workout controller `features/workout/workout_history.dart`; device rename `features/settings/devices_section.dart`.
- **(MD-2) A repository write with no character ops (snapshot) into a CRDT field open in the other device's editor is dropped there, and the next keystroke erases it** (BUG-220's pattern, seen with task notes). Real typing merges fine. Use real typing for anything that touches an open CRDT editor.
- **(MD-0) A sign-in seeds starters later than a sign-up:** right after sign-in B had no starter exercises / job stages / companies / workout plans for a few minutes, so an early `dbdiff.py` shows them as onlyA. Let both settle (or open the pages) before judging O1.
- **(MD-3) `voy.ps1` trims a step line**, so a `type` step's trailing space is lost ("PRE " types "PRE"). Put the space at the start of the next burst (leading spaces after `type ` are kept).
- **(MD-3) `vm field` truncates the text at ~120 characters**: compare long fields with `qa/steps/md-textcmp.py` (A, B and cloud: length + md5) instead.
- **(MD-3) Re-enable Verbose sync after every relaunch of either instance** (3.7 relaunched B and its uploads went unlogged until 3.19).
- **(MD-3) PowerShell variables are case-insensitive**: `$sql` and `$Sql` are one variable. A step script that did `$sql = $Sql.Replace(...)` waited on the wrong marker and turned "sequential" rounds into concurrent ones (that is how BUG-235 was first seen).
- **(MD-3) The red "Conflicting edits detected" banner shifts every page down ~100 px** (2880-wide coordinates): to-do panel checkbox (2097, 239), bell (2552, 354) once the task has a due date (the repeat button takes the old bell spot), Notes (2448, 547); journal Title (1843, 239), mood dots y 353. qa-068 shows it on both profiles until its 4 conflicts are resolved (MD-15.4).
- **(MD-3) The command-safety hook** also blocks `& $var …` (read as a git branch delete) and output redirection to a `$TMPDIR`-style path: call scripts by literal path, and write scratch output with the Write tool or a script.
- **(MD-3) Lists scroll**: MD3 entries 10+, dreams 10+ and tasks 15+ need `wheel <x> 900 -N` before `tap` (journal x 340, dreams 460, to-do 700); a task given a due date jumps to the top of its list.
- **(MD-4) Two-device writes at the same instant:** `python qa/steps/md4pair.py <lib> <body> [<bodyB>] [--delay-ms N] [--tag t] [--only qa|qb]` runs an evalc-style body on both profiles in parallel threads (`@W@` = qa/qb, `@TAG@`, `MARK;` logs each save's clock); a negative delay delays A. Also the generic "run one body on one profile" helper (`--only`). Saves ≥ ~200 ms apart are already sequential (the first change has arrived). Read results with `md4show.py <table> <id> <collection> <db cols> <cloud fields> [--wait S]` (row on A, B and cloud), `md4set.py <key[=column]>…` (settings + per-field stamps; its SAME/DIFF is fooled by list/float formatting, read the values), `md4rk.py` (ranking branches). The uid is hard-coded to qa-069 in `md4show.py` (`--uid` to change).
- **(MD-4) Force offline doesn't hold back settings saves** (BUG-242): to queue a setting offline, write a record first (its upload stalls the write gate for 45 s), then change the setting: it then waits in the outbox (`qa/steps/md04-43c2.sh`).
- **(MD-4) Verbose sync + a short task id = red To-Do page** (BUG-243): harness ids like `md4-tt` are under 8 characters. Either give dated tasks ids of 8+ characters or keep Verbose sync off (`qa/steps/md-verbose-off.dart.txt`); clearing the due date didn't help.
- **(MD-4) The command-safety hook** also blocks a python heredoc whose text holds backticks (read as command substitution), `printf … > file`, `powershell -File`, and `$P`-style command variables. Write files with the Write tool and put multi-step runs in a `.sh` file under `qa/steps/`.
- **(MD-4) `evalc` libraries for MD-4 bodies:** settings / snippets / dictionary `features/settings/settings_page.dart`; weather `features/settings/weather_location_tile.dart`; finance categories `features/finance/finance_category_modal.dart` (has `newId`); rooms `features/finance/finance_analytics_view.dart`; jobs `features/jobs/jobs_page.dart`. `newId` and `unawaited` aren't in scope in `settings_page.dart` / `trash_item_detail.dart` (use fixed ids and `.ignore()`).
- **(MD-5) Calling an app action that needs a `BuildContext` / `WidgetRef`:** `qa/steps/md05-dlg.ps1 -Profile p -Lib … -Body … -Tag t [-Tap <label>]` runs the body in the background (`md4pair.py --only`) and taps the confirm dialog's label on that instance. In the body, use the first mounted element under the Navigator that `is WidgetRef` (see `md05-del-journal.dart.txt`). Libraries: the action's own file when it imports Flutter widgets (`journal_list_actions.dart`, `todo_list_actions.dart`, `calendar_list_actions.dart`, `calendar_event_delete.dart`, `study_actions.dart`, `leetcode_actions.dart`, `finance_soft_delete.dart`, `rankings_actions.dart`); `workout_actions.dart` and `journal_entry_delete.dart` have no `Element` in scope, so use `workout_exercise_panel.dart` / `journal_page.dart`; `softDeleteTaskWithSubtasks` from `todo_page.dart`; remote purge `dev_remote_purge_tile.dart`. Trash restore / erase: `trashServiceProvider` (`md05-restore.dart.txt`, `md05-erase.dart.txt --tag "id1+id2"`). Typing on one device while the other acts: `md05-race.ps1`.
- **(MD-5) Record `copyWith` bumps the version itself** and has no `version` / `updatedAt` parameters (CalendarEvent, StudyCard; `bumpVersion: false` keeps it).
- **(MD-5) Startup is pull, then purge** (`SyncEngine.pullOnStartup`): to simulate an expired tombstone, age it (`qa/steps/md05-511-age.py`, app stopped) only after a launch whose pull already moved the watermark past the tombstone's upload; otherwise the pull re-applies the cloud's fresh stamps first.
- **(MD-5) Study and Workout sit below the rail's fold:** `wheel 88 1300 -10` before `tap Study` / `tap Workout`, `wheel 88 1300 10` to come back (`qa/steps/md05-ui.ps1` does this).
- **(MD-5) The command-safety hook** also blocks `mv` with a variable path, a PowerShell `foreach` that splits strings into `$a` and indexes it, and python heredocs holding backticks: use a script file or the Edit tool. `bash` from the PowerShell tool hits a WSL stub: run `.sh` files from the Bash tool.
- **(MD-6) Drag-reorders can't be posted:** `voy.ps1 drag` is one instant burst and starts no reorder (to-do list, planner). Call the page's own drop handler by eval instead: private members are in scope in their library, e.g. find the `_TodoPageState` element and call `_reorderActiveTasks` (`qa/steps/md06-61-reorder.dart.txt`, library `features/todo/todo_page.dart`); same for `_moveTaskToList`, the Journal page's `_moveEntryItemToJournal` (`md06-66-*.dart.txt`). Workout actions need `features/workout/workout_page.dart` (the only workout file importing providers, `WorkoutActions` and widgets); the full check `features/dev/dev_sync_compare_tile.dart`.
- **(MD-6) Saves released together can carry the identical microsecond stamp** (both processes read one Windows clock tick, ~16 ms): exact `updatedAt` / `createdAt` ties are likelier here than between real devices. Mention it when a result hinges on a tie.
- **(MD-6) A device's own writes by eval don't refresh its own page** (other devices' arrivals do): after a seed, invalidate the providers (`qa/steps/md06-inval.dart.txt`) or switch lists. Screen order of matching texts on both profiles: `qa/steps/md06-scr.ps1 -Q "<text>" [-N n]`. `md4show.py` / `md4set.py` read the uid from `MD_UID` (default still qa-069).
- **(MD-7) Helpers:** `MD_UID=<uid> python qa/steps/md07-cnt.py counter|ranking <id>` (per-device count rows on A, B and cloud, with sums), `md07-doc.py <collection> <id> [fields]` (one cloud doc, read-only; for tables without `updated_at`, which `md4show.py` needs), `md07-crop.py <out> <x0,y0,x1,y1> <shots…>` (same region of several shots stacked, to compare A and B at a glance). Table names: `leet_code_problems_table` / `leet_code_review_log_table`; `study_cards_table` has `ease` / `review_count`. Seed library for `md07-seed.dart.txt`: `features/trash/trash_item_detail.dart` (`deviceIdProvider` and `invalidateAllDataProvidersIn` are in scope there).
- **(MD-7) Grade buttons are disabled until the card is flipped** (a click on them does nothing, no error): click the card (1536, 900) or press Space first. The workout live view's Complete set sits at (1440, 1289) once a "Last …" line shows, (1440, 1247) without. A ranking category's Count stepper appears only with `parentCountEnabled` (Manage sheet; by eval `md07-72-enable.dart.txt`); the list row's "+" shows only on hover (fg).
- **(MD-6) The rail scrolls:** after `wheel 88 1300 -10` (Study / Workout) the top items are gone, and a later `tap Journal` misses silently in some runs; `wheel 88 900 10` first. A click at the journal switcher's (340, 55) on the Workout page toggles Week/Split.
- **(MD-8) Helpers:** `python qa/steps/md08-rule.py <qa|qb> <id> <title> <+N|HH:MM> [--kind once|daily] [--targets id,…] [--off] [--delete] [--body t]` creates/edits a scheduled rule through the repository + push and refreshes that device's rule provider (an eval write doesn't reach its own engine otherwise). `python qa/steps/md08-rem.py <rule id|source key> [--since HH:MM]` prints the delivery state and history on A and B (device ids shown as A/B, times local) plus Voyager's Windows toasts since that time (`wpndatabase.db`, read-only). Entity bells by eval: `setEntityReminder` from `features/todo/todo_edit_panel.dart` (tasks) or `features/calendar/calendar_event_panel.dart` (events; the to-do library has no `CalendarEvent`).
- **(MD-8) Both profiles' toasts for one reminder replace each other** (same `Voyager.App` AUMID and tag): Windows keeps one, so count `osFired` lines per device, not toasts. The sticky stack is hidden while the Inbox popover is open (close it with Esc before `tap Acknowledge`); stickies sit at (2728, 1719) Acknowledge / (2328, 1719) Snooze 10 min / (2533, 1719) Tomorrow, the next one 204 px up. The Inbox's quick-reminder field is at (552, 782) with no Hidden rows above (re-measure: the popover grows upward); emptying a pinned note deletes it; the feed row's ✕ is hover-only (fg).
- **(MD-8) `wait_row.py` on delivery logs:** a device stores its own `at` as local text and pulled rows as `…Z`, so a string `at > '…T20:58'` filter misses the writer's own rows; filter on `event_type` / `device_id` instead.
- **(MD-9) Helpers:** `python qa/steps/md09-count.py` (live/deleted md9 rows per kind + outbox on A and B), `MD_UID=<uid> [MD9_PREFIX=md9b-] python qa/steps/md09-cloud.py [--all]` (each md9 record's version / deleted / text on A, B and cloud), `md09-ops.dart.txt` with tag `<label>_<NNo…>` (o = e edit / d delete / n new, applied to all five kinds; run via `md4pair.py … --only`), `bash qa/steps/md09-91.sh` (A offline, both write, A back online, timed; `ORDER=ba` for B first), `md09-wr.sh` (one ops tag timed on the receiver), `md09-o6.sh` (O6 on the latest run log of each profile), `md09-98-mon.py <secs>` (A's versions every 100 ms, flags drops), `md09-99-age.py <prof> [days]` (outbox `added_at`, app stopped), `md09-97-state.dart.txt` (connectivity, write gate, outbox as one log line).
- **(MD-9) The connectivity probe runs every 60 s online and needs two failures:** after Force offline the app only notices (badge, and the reconnect `pullAll` on the way back) 60–70 s later. A shorter hold reconnects through Firestore alone with no catch-up pull, which changes outcomes (BUG-256's two shapes). Hold ≥ 75 s when a test needs the reconnect path.
- **(MD-9) Offline pushes reach the outbox only after the write gate's 45 s timeout;** until then `pending_uploads_table` reads 0 although the edits are unsent (they sit in Firestore's own queue). Wait ≥ 50 s before judging an offline outbox.
- **(MD-9) `vm find` / `tap` break with "Infinity or NaN toInt" once Settings → Appearance has been opened** (a kept-alive sliver with NaN geometry; the walker in `vm.dart` rounds it), on every page until a relaunch. Use coordinates (Navigation pages (576, 1572); its Dev eye (1623, 1570), Save (1709, 1675); Dev on the rail (88, 1404) after `wheel 88 1300 -10`). Scroll the Dev page's backlog tile into view with `qa/steps/md09-backlog-show.dart.txt` (library `features/dev/dev_page.dart`; the Dev page must be open).
- **(MD-10) Helpers:** `python qa/steps/md10-age-wm.py <prof> <days> [--both] [--show]` (app stopped: moves every sync watermark's `last_full_pull_at` back; 8 days forces a weekly full pull, 31+ trips `catchUpIfAway`), `MD_UID=<uid> python qa/steps/md10-cloudpoll.py <secs> <coll>/<id>[:field] …` (prints each change of version / deleted / field in the cloud, ~0.3 s; catches transient overwrites a before/after snapshot misses). `md09-cloud.py` / `md09-count.py` take the `md10-` prefix (`MD9_PREFIX=md10-`, `md09-count.py md10-`).
- **(MD-10) A startup pull on a small account (~3 s) ends before `launch.ps1` has attached**, so nothing can be done "during" it by harness; use qa-026 (cold sign-in ~30 s, or an 8-day aged full pull 31–39 s). `launch.ps1` inside a bash script blocks on its pipe (the attach inherits stdout): call it from the PowerShell tool. A relaunched profile comes up unparked at 2544×1591 (the Journal body click (1843, 1008) still lands), so `park` again; and re-enable Verbose sync after every launch.
- **(MD-10) The Journal page reopens its top entry at launch,** not the last one viewed (only `last_viewed_journal_id`, the journal, is stored).
- **(MD-10) Two `voy.ps1` runs typing at once (`together.ps1`) can flip letter case** in bursts ("b103 moRE", "ZK#"): the bg Shift workaround is shared. Type lowercase-only words in concurrent runs and don't read case changes as app bugs.
- **(MD-9) Stall log in a profile is off** (no `perf_stall.enabled` marker): turn it on per instance with `vm.exe eval core/dev/perf_stall_logger.dart "PerfStallLogger.instance.setEnabled(true)"` (VOYAGER_QA_PROFILE set); it writes `<profile>\logs\perf_stall.log`.
- **(MD-11) Helpers:** `python qa/steps/md11-attach.py <qa|qb> <collection> <docId> <label> [--facet front|back] [--file png]` attaches a labelled PNG (or a file's bytes) through `MediaService.attachBytes` (library `core/media/widgets/media_attach.dart`: it has `Uint8List`, providers and `MediaFacet`; `settings_page.dart` / `media_storage_dialog.dart` lack `Uint8List`). `MD_UID=<uid> python qa/steps/md11-state.py [<docId>|all] [--no-cloud]` prints references and assets on A, B and cloud, the local file and the Storage object (GCS JSON API on bucket `voyager-db9de.firebasestorage.app`, metadata only). `md11-online.py <prof> <secs>` prints changes of the app's `isOnline`; `md11-age.py <prof> <days> <mediaIdPrefixes>` ages media clocks (app stopped). Media switches: `md11-set.dart.txt` with tag `up_0|up_1|down_0|down_1|pre_0|pre_1` (`features/settings/settings_page.dart`). Bugs: `python qa/steps/md-addbug.py <entries.txt>` appends CRLF entries to `BUGS.md` and moves its Last ID marker.
- **(MD-11) A parked profile never probes connectivity:** `ConnectivityStatusController` only probes while the app lifecycle is `resumed`, and a profile launched by `launch.ps1` (start-parked change) is never activated, so Force offline never turns it "offline" (no badge, no reconnect resume / `pullAll`). A profile signed in by `session_start.ps1` / `login.ps1` was activated once by the fg login typing. For a test that needs the offline→online edge, run `qa/steps/md11-resumed.dart.txt` (library `features/dev/dev_page.dart`) on that profile first (simulated focus), and hold ≥ 60 s.
- **(MD-11) Media rows arrive without a refresh:** an image from the other device shows only once its owner is (re)opened (BUG-260), and its bytes are fetched only when rendered or at the next launch (BUG-263). Reselect the entry / task before a UI check; read `download_state` rather than assuming bytes.
- **(MD-12) Helpers and switching:** switch accounts by a real Settings → Account → Sign out (`voy.ps1 do "wheel 88 1300 -10; wait 400; tap Settings; wait 1200; tap Account; wait 1000; tap Sign out"`), then `login.ps1 -Profile x -Email …` (`-Password` for a changed one). If the store has queued or parked rows, a "Discard unsynced changes?" dialog waits after the login (`login.ps1` doesn't see it; `whoami` already reports the new account while the store is still the old one's): Cancel (2396, 1045), "Discard and sign in" (2612, 1045). After any switch, restart the instance before trusting Workout / Jobs or `dbdiff.py` (BUG-266: starters missing). After a sign-out with unacknowledged writes, restart before testing sync again (BUG-267). Change password dialog fields at (1440, 768 / 887 / 1007), its Change button (1736, 1133) (`tap Change` hits the list row). New: `qa/steps/md12-drain.py "<title prefix>"` (uploaded / queued / lost after a sign-out mid-drain), `md12-xtalk.py <foreign on qa> <foreign on qb>` (foreign ids in every table). `md-q.py`'s outbox columns: `document_id`, `collection_name`, `added_at`, `failure_reason`.
- **(MD-12) The command-safety hook** also blocks PowerShell calls that mix `$var=Get-Date` arithmetic or `"$h\script.ps1"` with harness calls: print `Get-Date -Format HH:mm:ss.fff` between steps and call scripts by their full literal path.
- **(MD-12) A sign-up can leave an account with a password other than the one typed** (qa-076: `login.ps1` read the field back as `qavoyager2026`, yet that password never worked). The Windows Firebase SDK reports a wrong password as `[firebase_auth/unknown-error] An internal error has occurred.` (the login page: "Sign in failed. Check your email and password"), so it looks like a backend fault. After any `-SignUp`, prove the password at once by signing B in with `login.ps1 -Profile qb` before seeding anything; if it fails, retire the account and sign up the next number. Don't probe for the actual password: the permission classifier refuses it as credential exploration. App output (`debugPrint`) lands in `run-<profile>-*.exe.log`, not the attach `.log`.
- **(MD-11) The three image switches sync** (uploads, downloads, background prefetch): turning one off on A turns it off on B within seconds; restore all three before the next section.
- **(MD-13) Helpers:** force a verified automatic backup (there's no "Back up now" for them) with `python qa/steps/md4pair.py features/dev/dev_backup_failure_tile.dart qa/steps/md13-backup.dart.txt --tag <t> --only qa|qb` (Dev's simulated-failure-off path; prints `MD13BK <files>` in the run log). Answer a profile's Windows Open dialog (Import Backup) without global input: click Import, then `qa/steps/md13-filedlg.ps1 -Profile qa -Path <full path>` (finds the `#32770` of that profile's PID, WM_SETTEXT + BM_CLICK; `-Cancel` too). `md13-eve.dart.txt` tag `add|del` (an evening expense).
- **(MD-13) Backup list coordinates (maximized, Settings → Data after `wheel 1500 900 -10`):** status row (475, 1322) opens the list; first row's ⋯ (1958, 559) → Restore… (1765, 565); rows step 128 px (third row ⋯ (1958, 815)); confirm's Restore (2460, 985); list Close (1945, 1364); Import Backup (445, 1688). **The list puts every "Before restore" snapshot above the automatic backups**, so the top row is often a snapshot: read the confirm dialog's "since <time>" before clicking Restore. `find`/`tap` break once Settings is opened (MD-9 quirk): use coordinates in Settings and in the Journal list afterwards (entries in list order, step 168 px).
- **(MD-13) After any restore, don't trust journal bodies on the restoring device once the other device types** (BUG-270), and close the other device's editor before restoring unless that's the test (BUG-271). A cold re-sign-in shows what the cloud really yields for a body.
- **(MD-14) Counting weather requests.** The app logs no weather request (only `[weather] warmup took …`). Server side: `gcloud logging read 'resource.type="cloud_run_revision" AND httpRequest.requestUrl:"refreshWeather" AND timestamp>="<UTC>"' --project voyager-db9de --format='value(timestamp,httpRequest.requestUrl)'` lists every `refreshWeather` / `refreshWeatherForecast` call. It covers all accounts, so make sure Juno's install isn't running. Per instance: `httpprof.py`. Lock claims and releases print as `Transaction set…` / `Transaction delete…/sync_locks/weather_fetch` in the run log; a refused claim prints nothing. Clients can't write `weather/*` (rules), so a stale cache needs the real 15 minutes. Both running instances then refresh on their one-minute timers as soon as it turns stale.
- **(MD-14) Don't launch two profiles at the same instant through `launch.ps1`.** Two concurrent `flutter attach` runs both hung at flutter's startup (no VM URI written), and ending the PowerShell jobs that started them also ended both exes. Launch one after the other (~10 s each).
- **(MD-15) Helpers:** `qa/steps/md15-152-age.py <prof> [--show]` (ages the MD-15 tombstones' `deleted_at` 40 days with the app **running**: a relaunch's own purge would take them first), `md15-153-desync.py <prof> <entry> <title>` (a local-only title, nothing pushed), `md15-154-collide.dart.txt` tag `NN` (same version + `updatedAt`, new title, through the app's push: only the receiver raises hardMetadataCollision), `md15-154-plant.dart.txt` tag `gap_<id>` / `dup_<id>` (library `features/dev/dev_page.dart`, the one with `SyncOperation` + providers + widgets in scope; appends to `sync_operations` and rewrites the doc unchanged so both devices re-read it), `md15-restore.dart.txt` tag `id1+id2` (Trash restore by id), `md15-je-title.dart.txt`, `md15-ev-edit.dart.txt` (library `features/calendar/calendar_event_panel.dart`), `md15-jrnl-ren.dart.txt`. In an evalc body, build JSON quotes with `String.fromCharCode(34)`: a `"` is stripped, and a Python-written `"` arrives as a bare quote. `c.read(deviceIdProvider)` is a plain `String` (no `.value`).
- **(MD-15) The conflict banner shows on Journal (and the other data pages), not on the Dev page**, where `tap Resolve` finds it NOT-CLICKABLE. With no conflicts left, the journal Title is back at (1843, 137); (1843, 239) then hits the mood slider. Dev page bottom (after `wheel 1500 900 -200`): Purge (462, 1260), Delete all entries (458, 1388), Delete all events (468, 1516), Reset journals (397, 1708); their confirms at (1773, 985) / (1790, 985) / (2200, 985).
- **(MD-16) Helpers:** `python qa/steps/md16-seeds.py [qa qb] [--rows]` (per seed / built-in table: live/total, fixed-id rows, versions, duplicate names). The journal hotkey's in-app path has no posted-key route in a profile (Ctrl+Alt+J posted to the window does nothing): call `_openQuickJournalEntry` by eval (`qa/steps/md16-163-quick.dart.txt`, library `features/journal/journal_page.dart`, via `md4pair.py --only`); `md16-163-select.dart.txt` tag `<journal id>` selects a journal on the page the same way. `together.ps1` runs that start with `wheel 88 1300 -10; wait 400; tap Workout` can miss the tap on one side while the rail still scrolls: wait ≥ 1200 ms after the wheel. In Jobs, (2510, 235) is not the Manage button at 2880 wide (hit the search field).
- **(MD-15) Any DB change invalidates a profile's full check, a received one included:** with the peer live, run Dev → "Check, then quit" (or stop both) right before a `reset.ps1` that relies on the gate.
- **(MD-17) Helpers:** `python qa/steps/md17-soak.py [--minutes 60] [--offline-every 10] [--hold 100] [--start-round N]` (the soak: per round a real typed journal entry on the writer + one MD-2 spec with both devices on its page; Force-offline windows with blind writes on both devices and a catch-up check; log `qa/exports/md17-soak.log`, rows moved to `md17-latency.tsv` so MD-2's file stays clean; summary `md17lat.py`). `md17-blindx.py <prof> <n> <spec>…` (MD-2 specs written blind while that profile is offline, then the arrival check), `KEY=… WRITER=qa HOLD=75 [ONLINE1=0] [OFFV="2 3"] bash qa/steps/md17-cheat.sh` (BUG-279 repro), `md17-load.dart.txt` (1 entry/s for 10 min, via `evalc.ps1`), `md17-frames.dart.txt` (frame timings every 10 s as `MD17FT` lines in the run log, via `md4pair.py --only`, library `features/dev/dev_page.dart`; stops logging after 30 min, the callback stays until relaunch), `md17-cpu.ps1 -Profile p -Seconds s` (CPU % of one core / all cores, working set), `md17-fsmetrics.py HH:MM HH:MM [--step s]` (Firestore read/write/delete counts and listeners from Cloud Monitoring, project-wide, lag ~3 min).
- **(MD-17) With "All journals" selected, the button reads "New entry in Journal"** (`tap New entry` misses): click (488, 1736). Type the title, `wait 400`, then click the body (1843, 1008): without the wait the title's last word can land in the body. The journal / to-do / calendar "All" switchers need at least one journal / list / calendar to exist.
- **(MD-17) `perf_stall.log` repeats breadcrumbs:** each STALL report dumps the recent events, so one remote change appears many times with the same timestamp; count STALL headers or distinct timestamps, not lines. The file rotates (B's went from 13k lines to 7k).

## 6. Manual-only / out of scope

| Item | Status | Why |
|---|---|---|
| Juno's real account: signing in, changing anything, "Continue with Google" | **MANUAL-ONLY — do not execute** | Real account / real Google OAuth |
| Login → "Forgot password?" **with an e-mail filled in** | **MANUAL-ONLY — do not execute** | Sends a real password-reset e-mail through Firebase. The empty-e-mail validation message is local and may be tested. |
| LeetCode username sync / stats fetch (`leetcode.com/graphql`) | **MANUAL-ONLY — do not execute** | Real external API. Leave Settings → LeetCode username empty. The "not configured" state may be tested. |
| Dev page → "Use direct OpenWeather" + API key | **MANUAL-ONLY — do not execute** | Calls OpenWeather directly with a real key |
| Links that open the browser (`launchUrl`: LeetCode/NeetCode, job URLs, OpenWeather credit, profile links) | **MANUAL-ONLY — do not click** | Opens Juno's real browser. Checking that the link exists/looks right is fine. |
| Importing any of Juno's real backups (in the local-only backup folder or `Downloads`) | **MANUAL-ONLY — do not execute** | Would copy real personal data into a QA account. Import only exports made by QA sessions. |
| Weather (Cloud Functions `geocodeLocation` / `refreshWeather*` → OpenWeather) | Allowed, low volume | Juno's own backend, read-only. Set at most a couple of test locations per session. |
| Firestore / Firebase Storage sync, device registration, reminders data | Allowed **only for voyager-qa-* accounts** | Isolated per account; this is the audit's isolation boundary |
| Settings → Change password (QA account only) | Allowed | Record the new password in the account table immediately |
| Settings → Start with Windows | Allowed **with restore** | Writes Juno's real HKCU Run entry. Before touching it, note the exact value (above). Afterwards restore it with `Set-ItemProperty 'HKCU:\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Run' -Name Voyager -Value '"C:\Users\Juno\AppData\Local\Programs\Voyager\voyager.exe" --hidden'` and verify. |
| Dev page → Remote purge / out-of-sync purge | Allowed only after `guard.ps1` OK | Deletes from the *current* account's Firestore |
| Demo page (`/demo`, debug-only playground) | Out of scope | Not shipped in release builds |
| Android / compact (<600 dp) layout | Out of scope | No Android SDK; compact width unreachable on desktop |
| Multi-monitor / mixed-DPI floater placement | Out of scope | One display attached |

**Added 2026-09-30 (outside a phase session):** fixes were made in the working tree for BUG-010/BUG-043 (every page now refreshes when the startup pull ends) and BUG-044 (a restore onto a wiped device no longer re-uploads what it pulled). A later change the same day speeds up the restore itself: a first pull reads every operation log in one paged read instead of one query per document (BUG-002's 2026-09-30 notes: measured 27.5 s against 62.1 s on Juno's account, with the same rows restored). See those entries' Notes. They're not yet checked in the running app: TEST_PLAN.md has a new **Fix verification (FV)** section covering these and every other fix made during the audit (FV-1…FV-11). Keep restarting after a cold re-login until FV-2 passes. BUG-004 isn't fixed by this change. *(Update, same day: FV-1 and FV-2 passed; see the Phase log and the note above.)*
